[日本語の取材へ](http://mtg-jp.com/eventc/gpniigata09/)

  
![](https://i.ormos.uk/9c/de/9cde62dd58622f4670df143d7652b49fdc83233171dce3e9d2a8a2ce026d5751.jpg)

Shingou Kurihara was a week removed from winning Grand Prix-Bangkok when he found himself back in the Grand Prix Finals hotseat. After going up a game in the match, he seemed like a shoo-in for the title. Tsuyoshi Ikeda, his last opponent, begged to differ. Overcoming a stumble on mana, Ikeda managed to come back from behind in order to take down the title. His blue-white deck featured powerful aggressive creatures, particularly those that had flying. Twin copies of [Illusionary Servant](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Illusionary+Servant) managed to avoid being targeted by anything from his opponents.

Tsuyoshi Ikeda is your 2009 Grand Prix-Niigata champion!

## Top 8 Player profiles

![](https://i.ormos.uk/87/66/87664d7f90d4f6a44058207b71479ac155f754e53cc74f086c19517cb4d224c6.jpg)

**Ren Ishikawa**  
**Current Residence:** Kanagawa Prefecture  
**Age:** 24  
**Occupation:** *Magic Online* player

**Tell us about your performance at the Grand Prix.**  
 • Day 1 (Sealed): 9-0  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Acidic Slime](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Acidic+Slime) and [Magma Phoenix](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magma+Phoenix).  
 • Draft 1: 2-1  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Green/Blue  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Elvish Piper](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Elvish+Piper) and [Enormous Baloth](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Enormous+Baloth).  
 • Draft 2: 1-2  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Mono-black with just a touch of blue.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Unholy Strength](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Unholy+Strength).

**What colors/archetype are you hoping to draft in the Top 8? What common card do you hope to get as your first pick?**  
 I would love to be able to kick off drafting green with either [Borderland Ranger](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Borderland+Ranger) or [Giant Spider](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Giant+Spider).

**Which *Magic 2010* card do you think is most underrated in draft?**  
[Elvish Piper](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Elvish+Piper).

**What was your hardest match-up/opponent in the Swiss rounds?**  
 I played a match with Katsuhiro Mori that really came down to one more turn.

**When did you start playing *Magic*?**  
 During *Fifth Edition*.

**What are your other significant *Magic* achievements?**  
 This is my second GP Top 8. I've also been in the Top 8 of Japan Nationals twice, coming in second one time.

**What are your non-*Magic* hobbies?**  
 I'm a devoted follower of the yaya3 and duotianshi MO Player of the Year Race!!

![](https://i.ormos.uk/df/24/df24eac5cd9394f5795b549cedf4b497f3aa4633abdff78bc540fda65893d059.jpg)

**Shingou Kurihara**  
**Current Residence:** Tokyo  
**Age:** 26  
**Occupation:** System engineer

**Tell us about your performance at the Grand Prix.**  
 • Day 1 (Sealed): 8-1  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Air Elemental](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Air+Elemental).  
 • Draft 1: 2-1  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Blue-white flying beatdown.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Air Elemental](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Air+Elemental).  
 • Draft 2: 2-1  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Green-white beatdown.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Serra Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Serra+Angel).

**What colors/archetype are you hoping to draft in the Top 8? What common card do you hope to get as your first pick?**  
[Blinding Mage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Blinding+Mage), leading off blue-white flying beatdown.

**Which *Magic 2010* card do you think is most underrated in draft?**  
[Ice Cage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ice+Cage).

**What was your hardest match-up/opponent in the Swiss rounds?**  
 Against Ryo Tasaki, the winner of the first Limits Championship.

**When did you start playing *Magic*?**  
 10 years ago.

**What are your other significant *Magic* achievements?**  
 I won Grand Prix Bangkok last week.

**What are your non-*Magic* hobbies?**  
 Watching movies on the Internet.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/d2/5b/d25b8da4c3a88c4ff4f8104f3cdd994dd843d21843d692fdf19a28a172df43cf.jpg)

**Tsuyoshi Ikeda**  
**Current Residence:** Fukuoka Prefecture.  
**Age:** 36  
**Occupation:** Card shop owner, pro player, father of two

**Tell us about your performance at the Grand Prix.**  
 • Day 1 (Sealed): 8-1  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Chandra Nalaar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chandra+Nalaar), Shivan Hellkite, [Siege-Gang Commander](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Siege-Gang+Commander).  
 • Draft 1: 2-1  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Blue-white.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Harm's Way](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Harm%27s+Way).  
 • Draft 2: 2-1  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Blue-red. Blue draw spells, red rares.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Chandra Nalaar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chandra+Nalaar), [Earthquake](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Earthquake), 2 [Mind Control](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mind+Control)s.

**What colors/archetype are you hoping to draft in the Top 8? What common card do you hope to get as your first pick?**  
 None, actually. I don't want to have to pick a common first.

**Which *Magic 2010* card do you think is most underrated in draft?**  
[Act of Treason](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Act+of+Treason).

**What was your hardest match-up/opponent in the Swiss rounds?**  
 Kentaro Yamamoto. He's a very clever player, and it was a true battle of wits. It's hard to get a read on him.

**When did you start playing *Magic*?**  
 When I was 25.

**What are your other significant *Magic* achievements?**  
 Pro Tour Top 4, lots of Grand Prix Top 8s. I was also on the national team in 2000.

**What are your non-*Magic* hobbies?**  
 Watching my kids grow.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/ec/1d/ec1d8cda5259ae84ac09a5d60b1cd6c35e4040b346a268f243793ccb1a759eb0.jpg)

**Hajime Nakamura**  
**Current Residence:** Tokyo  
**Age:** 24  
**Occupation:** Second-generation Mr. Planeswalker's Cup

**Tell us about your performance at the Grand Prix.**  
 • Day 1 (Sealed): 8-1  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Tendrils of Corruption](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tendrils+of+Corruption).  
 • Draft 1: 2-1  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Blue-white.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Armored Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Armored+Ascension)  
 • Draft 2: 2-1  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Red-white.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Dragon Whelp](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dragon+Whelp) and [Sparkmage Apprentice](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sparkmage+Apprentice).

**What colors/archetype are you hoping to draft in the Top 8? What common card do you hope to get as your first pick?**  
 Blue-white, starting with [Blinding Mage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Blinding+Mage).

**Which *Magic 2010* card do you think is most underrated in draft?**  
[Righteousness](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Righteousness).

**What was your hardest match-up/opponent in the Swiss rounds?**  
 Yuuya Watanabe. I was able to win only after two lucky topdecks.

**When did you start playing *Magic*?**  
 During 6th Edition.

**What are your other significant *Magic* achievements?**  
 None.

**What are your non-*Magic* hobbies?**  
 Cooking.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/f0/f4/f0f497cc42ef9f6a1bf2d2daf421ffec4c05d833b9620d0ebe2ae7e7d1513e46.jpg)

**Yuuya Watanabe**  
**Current Residence:** Tokyo  
**Age:** 20  
**Occupation:** First-generation Mr. Planeswalker's Cup

**Tell us about your performance at the Grand Prix.**  
 • Day 1 (Sealed): 7-2  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
 None in particular.  
 • Draft 1: 3-0  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Green-white beatdown.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Awakener Druid](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Awakener+Druid) and [Overrun](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Overrun).  
 • Draft 2: 2-1  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Mono-black.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Looming Shade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Looming+Shade).

**What colors/archetype are you hoping to draft in the Top 8? What common card do you hope to get as your first pick?**  
 Something with blue in it. It's an uncommon, but I would love top open a [Mind Control](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mind+Control).

**Which *Magic 2010* card do you think is most underrated in draft?**  
[Awakener Druid](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Awakener+Druid).

**What was your hardest match-up/opponent in the Swiss rounds?**  
 Masaya Kitayama. His deck was really strong, especially after sideboarding.

**When did you start playing *Magic*?**  
 During *Invasion*, when I was around 11.

**What are your other significant *Magic* achievements?**  
 This is my 3rd GP Top 8. I've been on the national team twice, and I was the 2007 Rookie of the Year.

**What are your non-*Magic* hobbies?**  
 Watching Japanime.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/8d/d3/8dd3ebcd952ed1b6d44f18e73d4c2d58e14accc05605ca5c78f0293d6dab7e43.jpg)

**Gaudenis Vidugiris**  
**Current Residence:** Madison, WI (USA)  
**Age:** 20  
**Occupation:** Law school student

**Tell us about your performance at the Grand Prix.**  
 • Day 1 (Sealed): 8-1  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
 Mind Contol and [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter).  
 • Draft 1: 2-1  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Blue-white.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter).  
 • Draft 2: 2-1  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Black-green  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Garruk Wildspeaker](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Garruk+Wildspeaker) (third pick!)

**What colors/archetype are you hoping to draft in the Top 8? What common card do you hope to get as your first pick?**  
 It depends on the pack. You just have to take the best common you see.

**Which *Magic 2010* card do you think is most underrated in draft?**  
[Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter). I've seen it come around as a 7th pick.

**What was your hardest match-up/opponent in the Swiss rounds?**  
 Yuta Ushie. I played him twice this weekend and lost both times.

**When did you start playing *Magic*?**  
 During *Onslaught.*

**What are your other significant *Magic* achievements?**  
 Finalist at GP Denver and GP Indianapolis.

**What are your non-*Magic* hobbies?**  
 Sports and traveling.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/4b/01/4b01fb117ac4fd3d1ae180f2b31569710dcda2b2400dcae7eb6b70d64ea5e105.jpg)

**Shou Ishikawa**  
**Current Residence:** Tokyo  
**Age:** 26  
**Occupation:** Saké shop owner

**Tell us about your performance at the Grand Prix.**  
 • Day 1 (Sealed): 7-2  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Dragon Whelp](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dragon+Whelp) and [Shivan Dragon](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shivan+Dragon).  
 • Draft 1: 3-0  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Blue-white angel stompy.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel).  
 • Draft 2: 3-0  
*What colors/archetype did you draft?*  
 Blue-red flying beats.  
*Which card(s) were your top performers?*  
[Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter).

**What colors/archetype are you hoping to draft in the Top 8? What common card do you hope to get as your first pick?**  
[Snapping Drake](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Snapping+Drake).

**Which *Magic 2010* card do you think is most underrated in draft?**  
[Stormfront Pegasus](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stormfront+Pegasus).

**What was your hardest match-up/opponent in the Swiss rounds?**  
 Shota Yasooka. I had trouble because his deck completely changed between Game 1 and Game 2. He's also a good player, always has a plan, and never gives you an opening. I lost due to a mistake I made with an attack, and it re-emphasized to me the importance of thinking about what you're doing.

**When did you start playing *Magic*?**  
 After *Tempest*.

**What are your other significant *Magic* achievements?**  
 I've won the Five Dragons Cup and the Planeswalker's Cup.

**What are your non-*Magic* hobbies?**  
 I like hanging out with my friends from the Ikebukuro Amenity Dream store. I'm a fan of pro wrestling and the Rakuten Eagles as well.

## Top 8 Draft with Gaudenis Vidugiris

*by Bill Stark*

A half dozen Americans registered to compete at Grand Prix-Niigata on Saturday morning, and by the start of the Top 8 draft, only one was left: Gaudenis Vidugiris. The Madison, Wisconsin resident also counts Lithuania as an ancestral home, and the coverage team leaned in to get a look at how he was going to tackle the Draft in the Top 8. Around him, seven hungry Japanese opponents looked to defend their home title; no non-Japanese player had won an individual Grand Prix title in Japan in years.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/a3/dc/a3dcd34412ebeed7c3fd465b62df12df3de4eef27e1a8aba4f15a1eb4143875b.jpg)The first pack offered up a [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt), a [Windstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Windstorm), and a [Bog Wraith](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bog+Wraith). Gaudenis flipped the cards around in his grip. The Bolt was clearly the best of the three, but it seemed apparent he was hesitant to go into red so early. Ultimately card quality won out with the Bolt being the choice, and he moved to his second pick. That offered up a [Howling Banshee](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Howling+Banshee), a [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter), and a [Snapping Drake](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Snapping+Drake). He selected the Merfolk, heading into red-blue, then picked up pick three. A relatively weak pack, he took [Canyon Minotaur](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Canyon+Minotaur) over [Essence Scatter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Essence+Scatter).

From there, the blue dried out pretty much completely. He took a [Giant Growth](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Giant+Growth) over a [Rampant Growth](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Rampant+Growth), then [Craw Wurm](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Craw+Wurm) over [Goblin Chieftain](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Chieftain). [Stone Giant](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stone+Giant) jumped into the heap at the expense of a [Viashino Spearhunter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Viashino+Spearhunter) and [Entangling Vines](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Entangling+Vines), and Vidugiris all but moved in to red-green as he took [Centaur Courser](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Centaur+Courser) over [Berserkers of Blood Ridge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Berserkers+of+Blood+Ridge). The pack rounded out with an [Ignite Disorder](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ignite+Disorder) and [Bramble Creeper](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bramble+Creeper), but the American was clearly frustrated that some very powerful black cards were coming around late. He had a look at multiple [Weakness](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Weakness)es, [Wall of Bone](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Bone), and a [Sign in Blood](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sign+in+Blood) well past when one would normally expect those cards to go by.

After abandoning his [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter), Gaudenis had scraped together a promising start to a red-green deck. His first pick out of pack two, however, did not contribute to that plan at all. [Captain of the Watch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Captain+of+the+Watch) beamed back from the rare slot, but the closest playable for his deck taht Gaudenis could come up with was a [Fiery Hellhound](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fiery+Hellhound). Rather than take the 2/2 so early, he opted instead to grab the Captain, keeping open the outside possibility he would wind up splashing a double white card. As if on cue, a [Borderland Ranger](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Borderland+Ranger) showed up for his second pick, making that splash even more possible. A late [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) showed up in third, indicating Gaudenis had set himself up well for the second pack successfully cutting red to his left.

[Seismic Strike](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seismic+Strike) fourth indicated that impression was correct, with a second one in fifth followed by a [Pyroclasm](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyroclasm) certifying it as fact. He then grabbed a [Stone Giant](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stone+Giant), bringing his total to two so far, as well as a second [Giant Growth](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Giant+Growth), a [Fiery Hellhound](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fiery+Hellhound), and late picks in [Goblin Piker](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Piker) and [Berserkers of Blood Ridge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Berserkers+of+Blood+Ridge). What had started off as a weak set of picks with nothing in his opened booster quickly loosened up into some very solid cards for Gaudenis. He had plentiful removal spells, he just needed to follow up with some creatures to seal the deal.

The third pack opened up on [Centaur Courser](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Centaur+Courser), though he gave a slightly more than longing glance at a [Serra Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Serra+Angel) left in the booster. Not to worry as he got a flier of his own as his second pick, nabbing [Dragon Whelp](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dragon+Whelp) over [Seismic Strike](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seismic+Strike) and [Royal Assassin](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Royal+Assassin). The 1/1 rare was flashy, but there was no reason to skew into an off-color when there were powerful cards left as actual options in Gaudenis' main colors. He had a chance at [Howl of the Night Pack](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Howl+of+the+Night+Pack), but already having picked up double [Seismic Strike](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seismic+Strike) opted to take the less green intense [Giant Spider](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Giant+Spider) instead. Just like that, a third [Seismic Strike](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seismic+Strike) showed up for him as his fourth pick, then [Rampant Growth](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Rampant+Growth) over [Fiery Hellhound](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fiery+Hellhound) and [Bramble Creeper](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bramble+Creeper). [Goblin Piker](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Piker), [Entangling Vines](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Entangling+Vines), two [Naturalize](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Naturalize)s, a [Mist Leopard](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mist+Leopard), and [Mold Adder](https://web.archive.org/web/20220127155427/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mold+Adder) joined the stack and Gaudenis was off to build his deck. Overall he had put together a solid stack of cards, though not in a color combination he preferred. He'd have to keep all of his wits about him to have a shot against the seven Japanese players thirsty to nab a home turf title.
